| A goal's runs are listed a page at a time, the ones still to end never folded away (`workflowRunsModel.paneWindow`); a queued run's place is said with the language's own ordinal | `views/_goal/runControl.test.mjs`, `scenarios/goals.test.mjs` |
| The retirement dialog's title follows the fate chosen inside it, as its button does; once it happened one sentence says the thing, its fate and what stopped with it — on the goal's page, in the designer and on the card — each kind counted with the catalog's plural | `views/_work/retireModel.test.mjs`, `views/_work/closeWorkstreamModel.test.mjs`, `scenarios/goals.test.mjs` |
| The goal's Details pane: the panel falls back to Details; the spend, the run's card, the projects' line and the origin's chips — a goal a run's step made has a door to that run — are the model's | `views/_work/goalInspectorModel.test.mjs`, `scenarios/goalInspector.test.mjs` |
| A capture is carried by the agents and teams the person picked in the dialog — never the core or a disabled agent, never a person, nothing handed over from the Teams screen — or by nobody, which leaves the Workflow Agent the whole enabled staff; the projects handed over with it are attached one by one — a refusal is logged and said once, with its reason, and the goal stands | `views/_work/newGoalModel.test.mjs`, `scenarios/goals.test.mjs` |
| A goal's status, its mode, a step's kind, the design card's kind and elapsed time and the queued chip are the catalog's words; the adoption's prefix is read in one place; the steps the list's *Act* acts on are the model's; a card that left while its verb's read was in flight is told nothing | `views/_goals/goalCardModel.test.mjs`, `views/_goals/goalStripModel.test.mjs`, `views/_goal/designStatus.test.mjs`, `views/_goal/proposalRouting.test.mjs`, `views/_goal/progressModel.test.mjs` |
| The run form: the line under a field says whether it must be given, what picking a project does — attached to the goal by the start, or where the steps work in a run of the workspace, which attaches nothing — and what is wrong, last; an account is picked among this machine's accounts of the input's connector, the default first | `views/_workflow/workflowForm.test.mjs`, `scenarios/workflows.test.mjs` |
| What listening needs is asked as required, whatever the input says of itself for a run by hand, and an input a default fills is never asked — on *Turn on*, on a goal's *Start listening…* and on its Workflow tab, by one rule (`startForm.listeningInputs`) | `views/_workflow/forms/startForm.test.mjs`, `views/_workflow/listeningModel.test.mjs`, `scenarios/workflows.test.mjs` |
| A refused save is a conflict only when the stored copy moved past the revision it was sent at; a 409 over a copy nobody changed is the node's own sentence beside the draft, tried again at the backoff's pace; a stored copy that could not be read is said in the log; the header's line is the catalog's in every state; the row is asked again once per revision | `views/_workflow/designerSession.test.mjs`, `scenarios/workflows.test.mjs` |
| A spawn step is asked what its child's workflow asks: a row per input, what its run needs and the step does not give named and drawn, what the child does not ask shown to be removed; on another workflow it keeps what that one asks too — and everything while that one's inputs are not read | `views/_workflow/forms/spawnStepModel.test.mjs`, `scenarios/workflows.test.mjs` |
| No step form turns a picker's word into an assignee itself: one model for a field that takes one or an input, and for a list whose input references ride behind the fixed ones | `views/_workflow/forms/assigneeRefModel.test.mjs` |
| A condition as the editor makes it: a fresh one names what exists and never a blank reference, a change of kind wraps what was there, a kind is offered only when it can be drawn, and what a value's field shows is what it would write back — the text `5` stays text | `views/_workflow/forms/conditionModel.test.mjs` |
| The workflow picker's options: a name with its problems counted, a value no group carries said as archived or as another goal's design, a template named by its slug until it is installed; *Use template* and the picker find the installed copy by one rule; a library section is headed by its tag, the untagged by the platform's word; a step's upstream is every step a flow leads from, never itself | `views/_workflow/workflowPickerModel.test.mjs`, `views/_workflow/libraryModel.test.mjs`, `views/_workflow/workflowGraph.test.mjs` |
| Every model has a test | every `*.mjs` under `src/` has a sibling `*.test.mjs` |

## Extension points

| To add… | Touch, in order | The gate that catches a miss |
|---|---|---|
| a screen | the `Route` variant, `href` and `section` in `router.ts` → a lazy `Screen` case in `App.tsx` → a `Sidebar` destination in `nav.ts` | `npm run build` |
| a step kind on the canvas | a row in `stepKinds.mjs` (`STEP_KINDS`, `blankStep`, `branchesOf` when it branches) → `STEP_KIND_ICON` → a form under `views/_workflow/forms/` → the `StepNode` arm → `runView.stepActions` → `goalStripModel.KIND_LABEL` and `progressModel.stepHolder` | `stepKinds.test.mjs` (reads the Rust enum), `runView.test.mjs` |
| a settings panel | `views/_settings/<Name>.tsx` → a `panel(id, label, blurb)` under its group in `settingsLink.mjs`'s `SETTINGS_GROUPS` and the id in `SettingsTab` (`settingsLink.d.mts`) → its glyph in `Settings.tsx`'s `GLYPH` and its `panel.id === …` arm → a sentence that names it says `settingsPath(id)`'s words | `settingsLink.test.mjs`, `scenarios/settingsPaths.test.mjs` |
| a bridge method for addons | a `METHODS` entry (its permission, its window flag) and a `paramsOf` arm in `addons/addonBridgeModel.mjs` → the `handleCall` arm and, for an effect, its `perform` arm in `addonBridge.ts` → the name and sugar in `addons/sdk/bisa-addon.js` and `bisa-addon.d.ts` → a row in `docs/reference/addon-api.md` | `addons/addonSdk.test.mjs`, `scenarios/addons.test.mjs` |
| a model | `x.mjs` + `x.d.mts` + `x.test.mjs` beside its component; a Rust mirror reads the Rust source in its test | `npm test` |
| a keymap command | a `COMMANDS` entry with a chord per preset (and `mac`, where a Mac's differs) and a `when` → a dispatch arm in `shortcuts.ts` (or, for a `files` verb, a `COMMAND_MENU` row in `useTreeMutations.tsx`; for a browser verb, the `BROWSER_COMMAND` arm and the bar's case; for a terminal's `typed` text editing, its bytes in `terminal/typedKeysModel.mjs`) → `node scripts/gen-keymap-docs.mjs` → the id list in [ide/15](../ide/15-keymap.md); the chord must not be a native menu key equivalent | `keymapModel.test.mjs`, `keymapDocs.test.mjs`, `shortcuts.test.mjs`, `check-keymap-docs` |
| a Tauri command | `#[tauri::command]` in `src-tauri/src/main.rs` and its `invoke_handler` → a typed wrapper in `terminal/session.ts`, `shell/*.ts` or `api.ts` → `capabilities/default.json` if it needs a plugin → `src-tauri/Info.plist` if it asks the OS for a grant that needs a usage string | `cargo check` in `desktop/src-tauri` |
| an API call | a function in `api.ts` in its section group, on a documented route | `crates/bisa-node/tests/it/routes.rs` |
| an icon | `ui/icons.ts`, in the namespace it belongs to | — |
| the platform's mark (a redraw) | a new `logo/logo.svg` → `just app-icon` (the raster set under `src-tauri/icons/`) → the About row and the empty landing show the component unchanged | `ui/platformMark.test.mjs` (the file is self-contained; the component shows it and spells no path; the recipe reads the same file) |
| a theme | `theme/themes/<name>.css` answering every role in a light and a dark block, `color-scheme` stated, the material roles answered (`none`/`0px`/`1` for matte or opaque; alpha in the surfaces and a blur for glass) → the id in `THEMES` (`shell/theme.ts`) and in the `appearance.theme` choice (`settings.rs`) → a family row in `FAMILIES` with its `material` — the canvas reads the same roles through `flow.css`, the panes frost through `material.css` | `theme/roles.test.mjs`, `theme/themes.test.mjs` (the contrast bars and the three id lists) |

---

## Tests

`npm test` = `node --test "src/**/*.test.mjs"`: a fact lives in a pure model with a test; components
are not tested; there is no DOM — the designer's canvas renders nothing under `node --test`, and its
facts (layout, graph edits, tones, vocabularies) are the models'. The plumbing has models too:
`apiModel.mjs` (the base, a failure's kinds, the read deadline, a loose file's refusal as the node's status — `looseRefusal`), `busModel.mjs` (the filter, the
frame, the retry), `routeModel.mjs`, `shell/storedPrefModel.mjs` (every remembered choice),
`shell/placeMemoryModel.mjs` and `shell/keptMemoryModel.mjs` (where a person was, and what every
screen keeps), and the
facts lifted out of components — `ui/tagSearchModel.mjs`, `i18n/format.mjs`,
`ui/dnd/collisionModel.mjs`, the palette's match and cap in `shell/quickOpenScore.mjs`. The guards
read the sources: `noHappyDom` (no DOM runner), `ui/imports` (libraries through the kit),
`localStorageKeys` (every key documented), `logDoor` (one log door, no silent catch — the models walked with the components, the two silences that stay named with the fact each rests on),
`deadExports` (every export used), `apiMembers` (every member of `api` called by a source), `declarations` (every model's `.mjs` and `.d.mts` name the same values — declare what you export, export what you declare). A journey is a scenario under `scenarios/`, one file each —
from the workbench and the terminals to the Board, a workstream from the project it is born in to
the card it becomes (`workstreams.test.mjs`), a file from its name in the index to its save and its
conflict (`files.test.mjs`), the git panel, a conflict, the
designer, a workstream's lifecycle, the palette, the routes, the addons, the footer's resources, the
node's wire, the Pulse, what reaches a person outside the window, a goal's life and a workflow's making. `cd desktop/src-tauri && cargo test`
covers the shell (scope parsing, the PTY registry, the sidecar's binary search, port and health probe,
the stats module's figures, the browser views' placement, the window's place — `window_state.rs`).

---

## What the desktop refuses to do

- supply a path or a program to the shell: it names a scope and an id, a harness id;
- persist the set of open documents in the webview (the node's layout has them), or a node's coordinates — furniture, not a place;
- remember a dialog's or a form's fields, a secret field, a message's pending attachments or an invitation code; send what it remembers to the node, or sync it;
- reinterpret a memory of another version, or open on a place whose thing is gone;
- render a terminal inside the routed screen, or through a portal: unmounting ends the PTY;
- import a UI library outside the kit, or a DOM test runner at all.
